% 习题讨论课
% 使用 Tectonic 或 XeLaTeX 编译。正文可在下方 document 环境中编辑。
% 图像以无损 PDF 图像流嵌入本文件，编译时无需另附图片文件。
\documentclass[UTF8,fontset=fandol,zihao=-4,a4paper]{ctexart}
\usepackage[top=19mm,bottom=18mm,left=21mm,right=21mm]{geometry}
\usepackage{amsmath,amssymb,bm,esint}
\usepackage{booktabs,array,float,caption}
\usepackage[hidelinks]{hyperref}
\hypersetup{pdftitle={习题讨论课},pdfsubject={电磁学课程讲义},pdfauthor={}}
\ctexset{
 section={format=\Large\sffamily\bfseries,beforeskip=3ex,afterskip=1.5ex},
 subsection={format=\normalsize\sffamily\bfseries,beforeskip=2ex,afterskip=1ex}}
\setlength{\parindent}{2em}
\setlength{\parskip}{0.35em}
\linespread{1.18}
\renewcommand{\thefigure}{29-\arabic{figure}}
\captionsetup{font=small,labelformat=simple,labelsep=quad,skip=6pt}
\setlength{\abovedisplayskip}{7pt}
\setlength{\belowdisplayskip}{7pt}
\setlength{\intextsep}{8pt}
\pagestyle{plain}
\raggedbottom

% 自包含原图排版命令，保留原始像素和比例。
\newlength{\PptWidth}
\newlength{\PptHeight}
\newlength{\PptTileHeight}
\newlength{\PptTileY}
\makeatletter
\newcommand{\PptDrawTile}[3]{%
 \setlength{\PptTileHeight}{#2\PptHeight}%
 \setlength{\PptTileY}{#3\PptHeight}%
 \edef\PptTileHeightBP{\strip@pt\dimexpr0.996264\PptTileHeight\relax}%
 \edef\PptTileYBP{\strip@pt\dimexpr0.996264\PptTileY\relax}%
 \special{pdf:put @Ppt#1 << /Filter /FlateDecode >>}%
 \special{pdf:put @resources << /XObject << /Ppt#1 @Ppt#1 >> >>}%
 \special{pdf:content q \PptWidthBP\space 0 0 \PptTileHeightBP\space 0 \PptTileYBP\space cm /Ppt#1 Do Q}}
\newcommand{\PptImageBox}[3]{%
 \begingroup
 \setlength{\PptWidth}{#2}%
 \setlength{\PptHeight}{#3\PptWidth}%
 \edef\PptWidthBP{\strip@pt\dimexpr0.996264\PptWidth\relax}%
 \edef\PptHeightBP{\strip@pt\dimexpr0.996264\PptHeight\relax}%
 \leavevmode\hbox to\PptWidth{%
  \vrule width0pt height\PptHeight depth0pt%
  \csname PptStream#1\endcsname%
  \global\expandafter\let\csname PptStream#1\endcsname\relax%
  \hss}%
 \endgroup}
\makeatother


\usepackage{enumitem}
\usepackage{xcolor,tikz,needspace}
\usetikzlibrary{arrows.meta,calc,decorations.pathmorphing}
\definecolor{ink}{HTML}{25323C}
\definecolor{sourceink}{HTML}{A34435}
\definecolor{fieldink}{HTML}{205D83}
\definecolor{surfaceink}{HTML}{4B7B77}
\tikzset{
 charge/.style={circle,draw=sourceink,fill=sourceink!6,minimum size=6mm,inner sep=2pt},
 point/.style={circle,fill=ink,inner sep=1.5pt},
 axis/.style={draw=ink!65,thin,-{Latex[length=1.7mm]}},
 force/.style={draw=fieldink,thick,-{Latex[length=2mm]}},
 aux/.style={draw=ink!45,dashed,thin},
 dim/.style={draw=ink!60,thin,{Latex[length=1.6mm]}-{Latex[length=1.6mm]}},
 source/.style={draw=sourceink,very thick},
 surface/.style={draw=surfaceink,fill=surfaceink!7}}
\newcommand{\ProblemDiagram}[1]{\DrawDiagram{#1}{0}{Q}{题图}}
\newcommand{\AnalysisDiagram}[1]{\DrawDiagram{#1}{1}{A}{解答分析图}}
\newcommand{\DrawDiagram}[4]{%
 \par\smallskip\noindent\begin{minipage}{\linewidth}\centering\refstepcounter{figure}\label{fig:L29:#1:#3}
 \begin{tikzpicture}[x=1cm,y=1cm,font=\small,text=ink,line cap=round,line join=round]
 \csname Diagram#1\endcsname{#2}
 \end{tikzpicture}\par\vspace{2pt}
 {\footnotesize\textbf{图~\thefigure\quad #4}\quad\csname Caption#3#1\endcsname\par}
 \end{minipage}\par\smallskip}
\setlist[enumerate]{itemsep=4pt,parsep=0pt,topsep=5pt,leftmargin=2.3em}
\newcommand{\Coach}[1]{\par\smallskip\noindent #1\par\smallskip}
\newcommand{\Trap}[1]{\par\smallskip\noindent\textbf{注意}\quad #1\par\smallskip}
\newcommand{\Task}[2]{\needspace{85mm}\subsection*{习题 #1\quad #2}}
\newcommand{\Answer}[2]{\needspace{75mm}\subsection*{习题 #1 解答\quad #2}}
\newcommand{\Worked}[2]{\needspace{85mm}\subsection*{例题 #1\quad #2}}
\newcommand{\Solution}{\par\needspace{70mm}\noindent\textbf{解}\quad}
\newcommand{\Key}[1]{}

\expandafter\def\csname CaptionQP1W1\endcsname{球面是绝缘面，电荷不重新分布。}
\expandafter\def\csname CaptionAP1W1\endcsname{按分段总场积分绘制的轴截面场线；长度以\(a\)为单位。}
\expandafter\def\csname DiagramP1W2\endcsname#1{\ifnum#1=0 \draw[source] (-1.732,0)--(0,-1)--(0,1)--cycle;\draw[aux] (0,1)--(1.732,0)--(0,-1);\draw[aux] (-1.732,0)--(1.732,0);\node[left] at(-1.732,0){$a$};\node[below] at(0,-1){$b$};\node[above] at(0,1){$c$};\fill[ink] (-.577,0) circle(.03);\fill[ink] (.577,0) circle(.03);\node[below] at(-.577,0){$A$};\node[below] at(.577,0){$B$};\else \draw[source] (-1.732,0)--(0,-1)--(0,1)--cycle;\draw[aux] (0,1)--(1.732,0)--(0,-1);\draw[aux] (-1.732,0)--(1.732,0);\node[left] at(-1.732,0){$a$};\node[below] at(0,-1){$b$};\node[above] at(0,1){$c$};\fill[ink] (-.577,0) circle(.03);\fill[ink] (.577,0) circle(.03);\node[below] at(-.577,0){$A$};\node[below] at(.577,0){$B$};\draw[aux,very thick] (-1.732,0)--(0,-1);\draw[force] (-.45,.4)--(.45,.4) node[midway,above,yshift=9mm]{镜像};\fi}
\expandafter\def\csname CaptionQP1W2\endcsname{\(B\)是\(A\)关于\(bc\)的镜像。}
\expandafter\def\csname CaptionAP1W2\endcsname{虚线标出被移除的\(ab\)，反射保持\(bc\)贡献不变。}
\expandafter\def\csname DiagramP1W3\endcsname#1{\ifnum#1=0 \draw[ink,very thick] (-2,0)--(2,0);\draw[ink,very thick] (-2,1.8)--(2,1.8);\node[left] at(-2,1.8){$+$};\node[left] at(-2,0){$-$};\draw[dim] (2.4,0)--(2.4,1.8) node[midway,right]{$ d $};\draw[surface] (-1.95,0) rectangle(0,.9);\node at(-1,.45){金属};\node[above] at(-1,1.8){$S/2$};\node[above] at(1,1.8){$S/2$};\else \draw[ink,very thick] (-2,0)--(2,0);\draw[ink,very thick] (-2,1.8)--(2,1.8);\node[left] at(-2,1.8){$+$};\node[left] at(-2,0){$-$};\draw[dim] (2.4,0)--(2.4,1.8) node[midway,right]{$ d $};\draw[surface] (-1.95,0) rectangle(0,.9);\node at(-1,.45){金属};\node[above] at(-1,1.8){$S/2$};\node[above] at(1,1.8){$S/2$};\draw[force] (-1,1.7)--(-1,1.2);\draw[force] (1,1.6)--(1,.5);\node[right] at(1.1,1){$E_2$};\node[left] at(-1.1,1.45){$E_1$};\fi}
\expandafter\def\csname CaptionQP1W3\endcsname{金属块覆盖一半面积，有效间隙\(d/2\)。}
\expandafter\def\csname CaptionAP1W3\endcsname{同电压的两个区域构成并联电容。}
\expandafter\def\csname DiagramP1W4\endcsname#1{\ifnum#1=0 \draw[surface] (0,0) circle(1.3);\fill[white] (-.5,0) circle(.35);\fill[white] (.5,0) circle(.35);\draw[ink] (-.5,0) circle(.35);\draw[ink] (.5,0) circle(.35);\node at(-.5,0){$q_1$};\node at(.5,0){$q_2$};\node[right] at(2.8,0){$q$};\fill[ink] (2.8,0) circle(.04);\draw[dim] (0,-1.6)--(2.8,-1.6);\node[above] at(1.9,-1.75){$r$};\node[left] at(-1.3,.5){$R$};\else \draw[surface] (0,0) circle(1.1);\draw[aux] (0,0)--(3,0);\draw[dim] (0,-.65)--(1.1,-.65) node[midway,below]{$ R $};\node[left] at(-1.2,.5){$ Q_s $};\fill[ink] (0,0) circle(.035);\fill[sourceink] (.4,0) circle(.035);\node[below] at(0,-.12){$q_0$};\node[above] at(.4,.15){$q^\prime$};\node[charge] at(3,0){$ q $};\node[below] at(2,-.15){$r$};\fi}
\expandafter\def\csname CaptionQP1W4\endcsname{内壁和外壁的总电量约束分别使用。}
\expandafter\def\csname CaptionAP1W4\endcsname{外部等效镜像只定义球外解，位置不按比例。}

\expandafter\def\csname CaptionQP1W5\endcsname{三个源段均以同一密度带电。}
\expandafter\def\csname CaptionAP1W5\endcsname{两个直段场相消，圆弧贡献沿右上方。}

\expandafter\def\csname DiagramP1T12\endcsname#1{\ifnum#1=0 \draw[ink] (0,0)--(2,0)--(2,2)--(0,2)--cycle;\draw[ink] (0,2)--(.8,2.8)--(2.8,2.8)--(2,2);\draw[ink] (2,0)--(2.8,.8)--(2.8,2.8);\node[charge] at(0,0){$ q $};\else \draw[ink] (0,0)--(2,0)--(2,2)--(0,2)--cycle;\draw[draw=fieldink,fill=fieldink!7] (0,2)--(.7,2.7)--(2.7,2.7)--(2,2)--cycle;\draw[draw=fieldink,fill=fieldink!7] (2,0)--(2.7,.7)--(2.7,2.7)--(2,2)--cycle;\draw[draw=fieldink,fill=fieldink!7] (.7,.7)--(2.7,.7)--(2.7,2.7)--(.7,2.7)--cycle;\draw[aux] (0,0)--(.7,.7)--(.7,2.7);\draw[force] (1.2,2.35)--(1.2,3.1);\draw[force] (2.35,1.2)--(3.1,1.2);\node[below] at(1.3,-.5){三个远面各有$q/(24\varepsilon_0)$};\node[charge] at(0,0){$ q $};\fi}
\expandafter\def\csname CaptionQP1T12\endcsname{示意图的顶点场本身有奇点。}
\expandafter\def\csname CaptionAP1T12\endcsname{立方体空间对称性等分三块外侧面。}
\expandafter\def\csname DiagramP1T13\endcsname#1{\ifnum#1=0 \draw[source] (0,0)--(2,0);\fill[ink] (3.2,0) circle(.04);\node[above] at(3.2,0){$x_0$};\node[below] at(2,0){$L$};\else \draw[source] (0,0)--(2,0);\draw[force] (3.2,0)--(4.1,0) node[right]{$E_x$};\draw[dim] (2,-.4)--(3.2,-.4) node[midway,below]{$\delta$};\fi}
\expandafter\def\csname CaptionQP1T13\endcsname{场点在棒的右端延长线上。}
\expandafter\def\csname CaptionAP1T13\endcsname{近端距离控制场的发散尺度。}
\expandafter\def\csname DiagramP1T14\endcsname#1{\ifnum#1=0 \node[charge] at(-1,-1){$ +q $};\node[charge] at(1,1){$ +q $};\node[charge] at(-1,1){$ -q $};\node[charge] at(1,-1){$ -q $};\draw[aux] (-1,-1) rectangle(1,1);\else \node[charge] at(-1,-1){$ + $};\node[charge] at(1,1){$ + $};\node[charge] at(-1,1){$ - $};\node[charge] at(1,-1){$ - $};\draw[axis] (-2,0)--(2,0) node[right]{$x$};\draw[axis] (0,-2)--(0,2) node[above]{$y$};\fi}
\expandafter\def\csname CaptionQP1T14\endcsname{电荷符号在相邻顶点交替。}
\expandafter\def\csname CaptionAP1T14\endcsname{势的首个非零远区项与\(xy\)成正比。}

\expandafter\def\csname DiagramP2W1\endcsname#1{\ifnum#1=0 \draw[ink,thick] (0,0) circle(1.3);\draw[dim] (0,-1.6)--(1.3,-1.6) node[midway,below]{$ r $};\draw[aux] (0,-1.3)--(0,-1.7);\draw[aux] (1.3,0)--(1.3,-1.7);\node[charge] at(1.3,0){$ -e $};\draw[force] (1.3,0)--(1.3,.65) node[right]{$\boldsymbol v$};\else \draw[ink,thick] (0,0) circle(1.3);\draw[dim] (0,-1.6)--(1.3,-1.6) node[midway,below]{$ r $};\draw[aux] (0,-1.3)--(0,-1.7);\draw[aux] (1.3,0)--(1.3,-1.7);\node[charge] at(1.3,0){$ -e $};\draw[force] (1.3,0)--(1.3,.65) node[right]{$\boldsymbol v$};\node[above] at(0,1.6){$\boldsymbol L:\odot,\quad\boldsymbol m:\otimes$};\fi}

\expandafter\def\csname DiagramP2W2\endcsname#1{\ifnum#1=0 \node at(-.8,0){$\otimes I$};\node at(.8,0){$\odot I$};\draw[ink,->] (-.8,.6) arc(90:420:.6);\draw[ink,->] (.8,.6) arc(90:420:.6);\draw[ink,->] (0,1) arc(90:420:1.7 and 1);\draw[ink,->] (2.5,1.2) arc(90:420:.3);\node[above] at(-.8,.7){$L_1$};\node[above] at(.8,.7){$L_2$};\node[below] at(0,-1.1){$L_3$};\node[right] at(2.9,.9){$L_4$};\else \node at(-.8,0){$\otimes I$};\node at(.8,0){$\odot I$};\draw[ink,->] (-.8,.6) arc(90:420:.6);\draw[ink,->] (.8,.6) arc(90:420:.6);\draw[ink,->] (0,1) arc(90:420:1.7 and 1);\draw[ink,->] (2.5,1.2) arc(90:420:.3);\node[above] at(-.8,.7){$L_1$};\node[above] at(.8,.7){$L_2$};\node[below] at(0,-1.1){$L_3$};\node[right] at(2.9,.9){$L_4$};\node[below,align=center] at(0,-1.6){$-\mu_0I,\ +\mu_0I,\ 0,\ 0$};\fi}

\expandafter\def\csname DiagramP2W3\endcsname#1{\ifnum#1=0 \draw[source,->] (0,1.6) arc(90:360:1.6);\draw[source] (1.6,0)--(.7,0);\draw[source,->] (.7,0) arc(0:90:.7);\draw[source] (0,.7)--(0,1.6);\node[below left] at(0,0){$O$};\node[below] at(1.6,0){$R$};\node[left] at(0,.7){$r$};\else \draw[source,->] (0,1.6) arc(90:360:1.6);\draw[source] (1.6,0)--(.7,0);\draw[source,->] (.7,0) arc(0:90:.7);\draw[source] (0,.7)--(0,1.6);\node[below left] at(0,0){$O$};\node[below] at(1.6,0){$R$};\node[left] at(0,.7){$r$};\node at(0,-.55){$B_z:\odot$};\fi}

\expandafter\def\csname CaptionAP2W3\endcsname{两弧的圆心场同向，径向连接段的叉乘为零。}
\expandafter\def\csname DiagramP2W4\endcsname#1{\ifnum#1=0 \draw[surface] (0,0) rectangle(3,1.5);\draw[force] (-.8,.75)--(-.1,.75) node[above]{$I:+x$};\node at(1.5,.75){$B:+z\ (\odot)$};\node[above] at(1.5,1.5){$A$};\node[below] at(1.5,0){$A^{\prime}$};\draw[dim] (3.4,0)--(3.4,1.5) node[midway,right]{$b$};\node[below] at(.5,-.55){厚度a};\else \draw[surface] (0,0) rectangle(3,1.5);\draw[force] (-.8,.75)--(-.1,.75) node[above]{$I:+x$};\node at(1.5,.75){$B:+z\ (\odot)$};\node[above] at(1.5,1.5){$A$};\node[below] at(1.5,0){$A^{\prime}$};\draw[dim] (3.4,0)--(3.4,1.5) node[midway,right]{$b$};\node[below] at(.5,-.55){厚度a};\draw[force] (2,.6)--(2,1.3) node[right]{$E_y>0$};\node[right] at(3.6,1.4){$q>0$};\fi}

\expandafter\def\csname DiagramP2W8\endcsname#1{\ifnum#1=0 \draw[surface] (-1.2,-.7) rectangle(1.2,.7);\draw[axis] (-1.8,0)--(1.8,0) node[right]{$x$};\draw[axis] (0,-1.2)--(0,1.5) node[above]{$y$};\node at(-.6,.4){$\odot j_z$};\node at(.6,.4){$\odot j_z$};\node[below] at(-1.2,-.8){$-d$};\node[below left] at(-.05,-.8){$0$};\node[below] at(1.2,-.8){$d$};\else \draw[axis] (0,0)--(4.3,0) node[right]{$ x/d $};\draw[axis] (2,-1.4)--(2,2.6) node[above]{$ B_y/(\mu_0jd) $};\draw[source] (0,-1)--(1,-1)--(3,1)--(4,1);\draw[aux] (2,-1.4)--(2,1.5);\node[below] at(1,-1.4){$-1$};\node[below left] at(1.95,-1.4){$0$};\node[below] at(3,-1.4){$1$};\fi}
\expandafter\def\csname CaptionQP2W8\endcsname{电流沿\(+z\)，不是沿纸面长边。}
\expandafter\def\csname CaptionAP2W8\endcsname{纵轴处为\(x=0\)；层内磁场线性，层外为常量。}
\expandafter\def\csname DiagramP2W9\endcsname#1{\ifnum#1=0 \draw[ink,thick] (0,0) circle(1.3);\draw[dim] (0,-1.6)--(1.3,-1.6) node[midway,below]{$ R $};\draw[aux] (0,-1.3)--(0,-1.7);\draw[aux] (1.3,0)--(1.3,-1.7);\node at(-.35,-.35){$\odot\,B$};\draw[source] (0,0)--(.92,.92);\node[right] at(.95,.95){$P$};\draw[force] (.92,.92)--(.25,1.59) node[above]{$\boldsymbol v$};\else \draw[ink,thick] (0,0) circle(1.3);\draw[dim] (0,-1.6)--(1.3,-1.6) node[midway,below]{$ R $};\draw[aux] (0,-1.3)--(0,-1.7);\draw[aux] (1.3,0)--(1.3,-1.7);\node at(-.35,-.35){$\odot\,B$};\draw[source] (0,0)--(.92,.92);\node[right] at(.95,.95){$P$};\draw[force] (.92,.92)--(.25,1.59) node[above]{$\boldsymbol v$};\draw[source,->] (.92,.92)--(1.6,1.6) node[right]{$dF_B$};\draw[force] (.92,.92)--(.4,.4) node[left]{$T\,d\theta$};\fi}
\expandafter\def\csname CaptionQP2W9\endcsname{正电荷顺逆时针与磁场法向按右手规则定义。}
\expandafter\def\csname CaptionAP2W9\endcsname{磁力向外，保持同角速度需增加向内张力。}
\expandafter\def\csname DiagramP2W10\endcsname#1{\ifnum#1=0 \draw[surface] (-1.7,-.65) rectangle(1.7,.65);\foreach \x in {-1.4,-1,-.6,-.2,.2,.6,1,1.4}{\draw[ink] (\x,-.65)--(\x,.65);}\draw[axis] (-2.2,0)--(2.4,0) node[right]{$z$};\node[above] at(0,.7){$n,I$};\else \draw[surface] (-1.7,-.65) rectangle(1.7,.65);\foreach \x in {-1.4,-1,-.6,-.2,.2,.6,1,1.4}{\draw[ink] (\x,-.65)--(\x,.65);}\draw[axis] (-2.2,0)--(2.4,0) node[right]{$z$};\node[above] at(0,.7){$n,I$};\draw[force] (-1.2,.2)--(1.2,.2);\node[below] at(0,-.9){$B_{\rm in}=\mu_0nI,\ B_{\rm out}=0$};\fi}
\expandafter\def\csname CaptionQP2W10\endcsname{圆柱面电流是本题的严格理想模型。}
\expandafter\def\csname CaptionAP2W10\endcsname{内外常量的差由面电流给出，外场另由边界条件固定。}
\expandafter\def\csname DiagramP2W11\endcsname#1{\ifnum#1=0 \draw[source,->] (0,-.5)--(0,2.5);\draw[ink,->] (1,0)--(1,2)--(2.5,2)--(2.5,0)--cycle;\node[above] at(0,2.5){$I_s$};\node[below] at(1,0){$x$};\node[right] at(1.1,1){$I$};\draw[dim] (1,-.5)--(2.5,-.5) node[midway,below]{$a$};\draw[dim] (2.9,0)--(2.9,2) node[midway,right]{$b$};\draw[sourceink,-{Latex},thick] (1,.3)--(1,1.3);\else \draw[source,->] (0,-.5)--(0,2.5);\draw[ink] (1,0) rectangle(2.5,2);\draw[force] (1,1)--(.3,1);\draw[force] (2.5,1)--(2.9,1);\node[above] at(1.5,2.1){$B:\otimes$};\node[right] at(1.1,1){$I$};\draw[dim] (1,-.5)--(2.5,-.5) node[midway,below]{$a$};\draw[dim] (2.9,0)--(2.9,2) node[midway,right]{$b$};\draw[ink,->] (1,.3)--(1,.8);\draw[sourceink,-{Latex},thick] (1,.3)--(1,.8);\draw[sourceink,-{Latex},thick] (2.5,1.7)--(2.5,1.2);\fi}
\expandafter\def\csname CaptionQP2W11\endcsname{近边电流与长直线同向。}
\expandafter\def\csname CaptionAP2W11\endcsname{近侧吸引大于远侧排斥，总力向左。}
\expandafter\def\csname DiagramP2W12\endcsname#1{\ifnum#1=0 \draw[axis] (0,0)--(3.3,0) node[right]{$ x $};\draw[axis] (0,0)--(0,2.4) node[above]{$ y $};\node at(1,1){$B:\odot$};\draw[force] (0,0)--(.8,0) node[above]{$E$};\else \draw[axis] (0,0)--(2.5,0) node[right]{$x$};\draw[axis] (0,0)--(0,-3.5) node[below]{$-y$};\draw[fieldink,thick] plot[domain=0:360,samples=100] ({1-cos(\x)},{.5*(sin(\x)-\x*pi/180)});\fi}
\expandafter\def\csname CaptionQP2W12\endcsname{电荷从静止释放，先受电场加速。}
\expandafter\def\csname CaptionAP2W12\endcsname{摆线按\(x\)尺度\(v_{D}/\omega \)、\(y\)尺度\(2v_{D}/\omega \)绘制。}

\expandafter\def\csname CaptionAP2T1\endcsname{场方向按源线元与场点位移的叉乘确定。}

\expandafter\def\csname CaptionQP2T17\endcsname{两个同轴线圈的中心间距为\(2d\)。}
\expandafter\def\csname CaptionAP2T17\endcsname{曲线为精确同向轴场，中心消去二阶但保留四阶误差。}
\expandafter\def\csname DiagramP2T18\endcsname#1{\ifnum#1=0 \draw[surface] (0,0) rectangle(3,1.5);\draw[force] (-.8,.75)--(-.1,.75) node[above]{$I:+x$};\node at(1.5,.75){$B:+z\ (\odot)$};\node[above] at(1.5,1.5){$A$};\node[below] at(1.5,0){$A^{\prime}$};\draw[dim] (3.4,0)--(3.4,1.5) node[midway,right]{$b$};\node[below] at(.5,-.55){厚度a};\node[below,align=center] at(1.5,-1){$\sigma_0, R_H\longrightarrow n_e,n_h$};\else \draw[axis] (0,0)--(4.3,0) node[right]{$ D/S $};\draw[axis] (0,0)--(0,2.6999999999999997) node[above]{$ n $};\draw[fieldink,thick] (0,2)--(4,0);\draw[sourceink,thick] (0,0)--(4,2);\node[below] at(0,0){$-\mu_e$};\node[below] at(4,0){$+\mu_h$};\node[above] at(1,1.8){$n_e$};\node[above] at(3,1.8){$n_h$};\fi}
\expandafter\def\csname CaptionQP2T18\endcsname{迁移率已由独立测量给定。}
\expandafter\def\csname CaptionAP2T18\endcsname{允许区间内电子浓度下降、空穴浓度上升；各线用独立纵轴比例。}
\expandafter\def\csname DiagramP3W1\endcsname#1{\ifnum#1=0 \draw[ink,thick] (0,0) circle(1.3);\draw[dim] (0,-1.6)--(1.3,-1.6) node[midway,below]{$ R $};\draw[aux] (0,-1.3)--(0,-1.7);\draw[aux] (1.3,0)--(1.3,-1.7);\node at(0,.38){$\odot\ B$};\draw[source] (-1.3,0)--(1.3,0);\draw[ink] (-1.3,-1.3)--(1.3,-1.3)--(1.3,-2.2)--(-1.3,-2.2)--cycle;\node[left] at(-1.3,0){$A$};\node[right] at(1.3,0){$B$};\node[left] at(-1.3,-1.3){$C$};\node[right] at(1.3,-1.3){$D$};\else \draw[ink,thick] (0,0) circle(1.3);\draw[dim] (0,-1.6)--(1.3,-1.6) node[midway,below]{$ R $};\draw[aux] (0,-1.3)--(0,-1.7);\draw[aux] (1.3,0)--(1.3,-1.7);\draw[force] (1.3,0) arc(0:-150:1.3);\draw[source] (-1.3,-1.3)--(1.3,-1.3);\node[below] at(0,-2.4){$\mathcal E_{AB}=0,\quad\mathcal E_{CD}\ne0$};\fi}
\expandafter\def\csname CaptionQP3W1\endcsname{\(L_{1}\)上半圆链接半圆磁通，\(L_{2}\)在场区之外。}
\expandafter\def\csname CaptionAP3W1\endcsname{图示\(B\)增加时的顺时针感生场；零局部积分与零环流不同。}
\expandafter\def\csname DiagramP3W2\endcsname#1{\ifnum#1=0 \draw[surface] (0,0) circle(1.3);\node at(0,-.4){$B:\odot$};\draw[source] (-1.2,.5)--(1.2,.5);\node[left] at(-1.2,.5){$A$};\node[right] at(1.2,.5){$B$};\draw[dim] (0,0)--(0,.5) node[midway,left]{$h$};\draw[aux] (0,0)--(1.2,.5) node[midway,below right]{$R$};\else \draw[surface] (0,0) circle(1.3);\node at(0,-.4){$B:\odot$};\draw[source] (-1.2,.5)--(1.2,.5);\node[left] at(-1.2,.5){$A$};\node[right] at(1.2,.5){$B$};\draw[dim] (0,0)--(0,.5) node[midway,left]{$h$};\draw[aux] (0,0)--(1.2,.5) node[midway,below right]{$R$};\draw[force] (-.5,.65)--(.5,.65) node[above,yshift=12mm]{$E_x=h\dot B/2$};\fi}
\expandafter\def\csname CaptionQP3W2\endcsname{端点在磁场柱边界，\(h\)与\(L\)不是独立变量。}
\expandafter\def\csname CaptionAP3W2\endcsname{内部涡旋场的水平分量沿整根弦恒定。}
\expandafter\def\csname DiagramP3W3\endcsname#1{\ifnum#1=0 \begin{scope}[shift={(-3,0)}]\draw[ink] (0,0) circle(.7);\draw[ink] (-.7,0)--(.7,0);\node at(0,.3){$\odot$};\node at(0,-.3){$\odot$};\node[below] at(0,-.85){(a)};\end{scope}\begin{scope}[shift={(-1,0)}]\draw[ink] (.7,.12) arc(10:350:.71)--(.48,-.08) arc(350:10:.49)--cycle;\node at(0,0){$\odot$};\node[below] at(0,-.85){(b)};\end{scope}\begin{scope}[shift={(1,0)}]\draw[ink] (10:.7) arc(10:350:.7)--(350:.45) arc(350:690:.45)--cycle;\fill[white] (.507,-.0894) circle(.035);\draw[ink] (.483,-.117)..controls(.54,-.14)..(.535,-.06);\node at(0,0){$\odot$};\node[below] at(0,-.85){(c)};\end{scope}\begin{scope}[shift={(3.8,0)}]\draw[ink] (0,0) .. controls(-1.8,1.5)and(-1.8,-1.5)..(0,0) ..controls(1,.9)and(1,-.9)..cycle;\node at(-.8,0){$\odot$};\node at(.45,0){$\odot$};\node[below] at(0,-.85){(d)};\end{scope}\else \begin{scope}[shift={(-3,0)}]\draw[ink] (0,0) circle(.7);\draw[ink] (-.7,0)--(.7,0);\node at(0,.3){$\odot$};\node at(0,-.3){$\odot$};\node[below] at(0,-.85){(a)};\end{scope}\begin{scope}[shift={(-1,0)}]\draw[ink] (.7,.12) arc(10:350:.71)--(.48,-.08) arc(350:10:.49)--cycle;\node at(0,0){$\odot$};\node[below] at(0,-.85){(b)};\end{scope}\begin{scope}[shift={(1,0)}]\draw[ink] (10:.7) arc(10:350:.7)--(350:.45) arc(350:690:.45)--cycle;\fill[white] (.507,-.0894) circle(.035);\draw[ink] (.483,-.117)..controls(.54,-.14)..(.535,-.06);\node at(0,0){$\odot$};\node[below] at(0,-.85){(c)};\end{scope}\begin{scope}[shift={(3.8,0)}]\draw[ink] (0,0) .. controls(-1.8,1.5)and(-1.8,-1.5)..(0,0) ..controls(1,.9)and(1,-.9)..cycle;\node at(-.8,0){$\odot$};\node at(.45,0){$\odot$};\node[below] at(0,-.85){(d)};\end{scope}\node[below,align=center] at(0,-1.2){$(a)\ I_{\rm bar}=0;\quad(b)\ I=0;\quad(c)\ -2\dot\Phi;\quad(d)\ -\dot B(A_l-A_r)$};
\draw[force] (-2.3,0) arc(0:100:.7);\draw[force] (-3.7,0) arc(180:280:.7);
\draw[force] (1.7,0) arc(0:100:.7);\draw[force] (1.45,0) arc(0:100:.45);
\draw[force] (2.8,.27)--(2.55,0);\draw[sourceink,-{Latex},thick] (4.2,.4)--(4.4,.2);
\node at(0,1.3) {$\dot B<0$；蓝／红箭头为实际电流方向};
\fi}
\expandafter\def\csname CaptionQP3W3\endcsname{磁场只穿题设标出的区域；(c)是绝缘交叉的同向串联两匝。}
\expandafter\def\csname CaptionAP3W3\endcsname{减小正磁通时的环流方向，直径与C形带为零流，两匝同向、八字两叶反向}
\expandafter\def\csname DiagramP3W4\endcsname#1{\ifnum#1=0 \draw[surface] (-1.8,-.3) rectangle(1.8,.3);\draw[ink,decorate,decoration={coil,segment length=3mm,amplitude=3mm}] (-1.6,.1)--(-.4,.1);\draw[ink,decorate,decoration={coil,segment length=3mm,amplitude=3mm}] (.4,.1)--(1.6,.1);\fill[ink] (-1.6,.55) circle(.05);\fill[ink] (.4,.55) circle(.05);\node[above] at(-1,.8){$L_1$};\node[above] at(1,.8){$L_2$};\node[below] at(0,-.55){$M$};\else \draw[surface] (-1.8,-.3) rectangle(1.8,.3);\draw[ink,decorate,decoration={coil,segment length=3mm,amplitude=3mm}] (-1.6,.1)--(-.4,.1);\draw[ink,decorate,decoration={coil,segment length=3mm,amplitude=3mm}] (.4,.1)--(1.6,.1);\fill[ink] (-1.6,.55) circle(.05);\fill[ink] (.4,.55) circle(.05);\node[above] at(-1,.8){$L_1$};\node[above] at(1,.8){$L_2$};\node[below] at(0,-.55){$M$};\node[below,align=center] at(0,-1){$I_1=I_2:\ L_+;\quad I_1=-I_2:\ L_-$};\fi}
\expandafter\def\csname CaptionQP3W4\endcsname{黑点是同名端，图中的参考量采用同名端流入为正。}
\expandafter\def\csname CaptionAP3W4\endcsname{正、反串联改变交叉项的符号，总场能仍非负。}
\expandafter\def\csname DiagramP3W5\endcsname#1{\ifnum#1=0 \draw[ink,thick] (0,0) circle(1.3);\draw[dim] (0,-1.6)--(1.3,-1.6) node[midway,below]{$ R $};\draw[aux] (0,-1.3)--(0,-1.7);\draw[aux] (1.3,0)--(1.3,-1.7);\node at(0,.38){$\odot\ B$};\draw[source] (0,0)--(1.3,0);\fill[ink] (0,0) circle(.045);\fill[ink] (1.3,0) circle(.045);\draw[ink] (0,0)--(-1.8,0)--(-1.8,-2.3)--(-.65,-2.3);\draw[ink] (.65,-2.3)--(1.8,-2.3)--(1.8,0)--(1.3,0);\draw[force] (1.5,.1) arc(0:70:1.5);\begin{scope}[shift={(-0.65,-2.3)},rotate=0.0000]\draw[ink] (0,0)--(0.37,0);\draw[ink] (0.93,0)--(1.3,0);\draw[ink,fill=white] (0.37,-.13) rectangle(0.93,.13);\end{scope}\node[below] at(0,-2.45){$\mathcal R$};\else \draw[ink,thick] (0,0) circle(1.3);\draw[dim] (0,-1.6)--(1.3,-1.6) node[midway,below]{$ R $};\draw[aux] (0,-1.3)--(0,-1.7);\draw[aux] (1.3,0)--(1.3,-1.7);\node at(0,.38){$\odot\ B$};\draw[source] (0,0)--(1.3,0);\fill[ink] (0,0) circle(.045);\fill[ink] (1.3,0) circle(.045);\draw[ink] (0,0)--(-1.8,0)--(-1.8,-2.3)--(-.65,-2.3);\draw[ink] (.65,-2.3)--(1.8,-2.3)--(1.8,0)--(1.3,0);\draw[force] (1.5,.1) arc(0:70:1.5);\begin{scope}[shift={(-0.65,-2.3)},rotate=0.0000]\draw[ink] (0,0)--(0.37,0);\draw[ink] (0.93,0)--(1.3,0);\draw[ink,fill=white] (0.37,-.13) rectangle(0.93,.13);\end{scope}\node[below] at(0,-2.45){$\mathcal R$};\draw[force] (.3,.1)--(1.1,.1) node[above]{$I$};\draw[source,->] (.9,0)--(.9,-.65) ;\draw[aux] (.9,-.65)--(1.45,-1.12)--(1.95,-1.12);\node[right] at(2.05,-1.12){$dF_\theta$};\fi}

\expandafter\def\csname CaptionAP3W5\endcsname{径向电流在\(B\)中受反转动方向的力。}

\expandafter\def\csname DiagramP4W5\endcsname#1{\ifnum#1=0 \draw[ink,thick] (0,0) circle(1.3);\draw[dim] (0,-1.6)--(1.3,-1.6) node[midway,below]{$ R $};\draw[aux] (0,-1.3)--(0,-1.7);\draw[aux] (1.3,0)--(1.3,-1.7);\node at(0,1.9){$\odot\,\Phi_0$};\draw[surface] (0,0) circle(.35);\foreach \a in {0,45,90,135,180,225,270,315}{\node[charge,minimum size=3mm] at(\a:1.3){$+$};}\else \draw[ink,thick] (0,0) circle(1.3);\draw[dim] (0,-1.6)--(1.3,-1.6) node[midway,below]{$ R $};\draw[aux] (0,-1.3)--(0,-1.7);\draw[aux] (1.3,0)--(1.3,-1.7);\draw[force] (1.3,0) arc(0:85:1.3);\node[above] at(0,1.8){$\Delta L_z=Q\Phi_0/(2\pi)$};\fi}

\expandafter\def\csname CaptionAP4W5\endcsname{正链接磁通消失时，正电荷获得\(+z\)机械角动量。}
\expandafter\def\csname DiagramP4W6\endcsname#1{\ifnum#1=0 \draw[surface] (0,1.5) arc(90:-90:1.5)--(0,-.8) arc(-90:90:.8)--cycle;\draw[axis] (-.3,0)--(2,0) node[right]{$z$};\node[left] at(-.15,-.15){$O$};\draw[force] (.8,.4)--(1.4,.4) node[right]{$M$};\node[above] at(0,1.5){$b$};\node[left] at(-.15,.8){$a$};\else \draw[surface] (0,1.5) arc(90:-90:1.5)--(0,-.8) arc(-90:90:.8)--cycle;\draw[axis] (-.3,0)--(2,0) node[right]{$z$};\node[left] at(-.15,-.15){$O$};\draw[force] (.8,.4)--(1.4,.4) node[right]{$M$};\node[above] at(0,1.5){$b$};\node[left] at(-.15,.8){$a$};\node[above] at(2.3,1.2){外面$-M/6$};\node[below] at(1.2,-1.7){内面$+M/6$，底面$0$};\fi}

\node at(1,-1.8) {$B_z^{\rm in}-B_z^{\rm out}=\mu_0M$};\node at(4.8,0) {$\bm M\parallel +z$};

\expandafter\def\csname DiagramP4W8\endcsname#1{\ifnum#1=0 \draw[surface] (0,0) circle(1.3);\fill[white] (0,0) circle(.75);\draw[ink] (0,0) circle(.75);\draw[force] (-1,0) arc(180:265:1);\node[left] at(-1.4,0){$\boldsymbol M$};\fill[white] (-.12,.7) rectangle(.12,1.4);\draw[ink] (-.12,.75)--(-.12,1.3);\draw[ink] (.12,.75)--(.12,1.3);\node[above] at(0,1.5){$g$};\node[below,align=center] at(0,-1.8){$A_m,\ell_m;\quad A_g,g$};\else \draw[axis] (0,0)--(3.0999999999999996,0) node[right]{$  $};\draw[axis] (0,0)--(0,3.0) node[above]{$ W $};\draw[surface] (0.4,0) rectangle(1.05,2.1);\node[below] at(0.7250000000000001,-.1){$ |BH|V_m $};\draw[surface] (1.65,0) rectangle(2.3,1.05);\node[below] at(1.9749999999999999,-.1){$ W_g $};\fi}

\expandafter\def\csname DiagramP4W9\endcsname#1{\ifnum#1=0 \draw[surface] (0,0) circle(1.3);\fill[white] (0,0) circle(.75);\draw[ink] (0,0) circle(.75);\draw[force] (-1,0) arc(180:265:1);\node[left] at(-1.4,0){$\boldsymbol M$};\fill[white] (-.12,.7) rectangle(.12,1.4);\draw[ink] (-.12,.75)--(-.12,1.3);\draw[ink] (.12,.75)--(.12,1.3);\node[above] at(0,1.5){$g$};\else \draw[axis] (0,0)--(4.1,0) node[right]{$ -H_m $};\draw[axis] (0,0)--(0,3.0) node[above]{$ B $};\draw[fieldink,thick] (0,2.2)--(3.5,0);\draw[source,thick] (0,0)--(3.5,2.2);\fill[ink] (1.75,1.1) circle(.04);\draw[aux] (1.75,1.1)--(1.9,.7);\node[below] at(1.9,.6){工作点};\fi}
